# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
These AGENTS.md instructions replace all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions.

# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

point of the file.*

# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| Current local work in Claude/Codex cloud | `setup/github-sync/README.md`; private `codex/local-sync/<machine>/<checkout>` branches and generated `.cloud-sync/` metadata/conversation exports. Cloud edits use separate task branches. |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — intake queue; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies clips into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized, so inbox presence alone does not mean unprocessed |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **LSAT teaching:** use project-owned `lsat-u-voice` and `references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md`; this Joshua-selected standard takes precedence over older vendor voice blends.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.
- **Authorized cloud transfer (2026-10-07):** private sync branches also carry portable skills, memory and conversation exports. Joshua explicitly authorized credential transfer; implementation uses the authenticated encrypted package described in `setup/github-sync/README.md`; plaintext secrets and its unlock key stay outside GitHub and Drive. Cloud authentication and native browser sessions still require separate verification.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

## assistant

**Yes. You already have the tools for this, so you don’t need another extension.**

- **For you:** open a YouTube video, expand its description and click **Show transcript**. This works when captions are available. [YouTube instructions](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/15930243?hl=en)
- **For me, Claude Code or Antigravity:** use your installed **youtube-transcript-api** to save the entire available transcript as a local Markdown file with timestamps. It requires no paid API key. [Tool documentation](https://github.com/jdepoix/youtube-transcript-api)

You can simply say:

```text
Extract the full transcript from this YouTube URL.
Save it as a local Markdown file with timestamps.
Do not summarize it. Report any missing sections.
```

We already used this successfully for the Karpathy video. Videos without captions need audio transcription instead; automatic captions can contain mistakes.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
These AGENTS.md instructions replace all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions.

# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

## Shared Windows CLI tools

On Joshua's Windows laptop, `uv`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay`, and `deno` are shared Windows user installs. Read `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\references\ai-workspace\windows-cli-toolchain.md` for their paths, versions, usage, and PATH refresh steps. Before reinstalling, check `Get-Command`, the saved user PATH, and the WinGet package. A running IDE may have stale PATH; refresh the shell or restart that IDE. Keep Python dependencies in project environments.

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Shared Windows CLI tools for Codex, Claude Code, and Antigravity | `references/ai-workspace/windows-cli-toolchain.md` — installed paths, PATH refresh, and usage for `uv`, `yt-dlp`, FFmpeg, and Deno |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| Current local work in Claude/Codex cloud | `setup/github-sync/README.md`; private `codex/local-sync/<machine>/<checkout>` branches and generated `.cloud-sync/` metadata/conversation exports. Cloud edits use separate task branches. |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — intake queue; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies clips into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized, so inbox presence alone does not mean unprocessed |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **LSAT teaching:** use project-owned `lsat-u-voice` and `references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md`; this Joshua-selected standard takes precedence over older vendor voice blends.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Shared Windows command tools:** use `uv`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay`, and `deno` from the Windows user PATH. For versions, locations, how to invoke them, or a stale terminal environment, read `references/ai-workspace/windows-cli-toolchain.md`. Check before reinstalling; restart the affected IDE when its inherited PATH is stale.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.
- **Authorized cloud transfer (2026-10-07):** private sync branches also carry portable skills, memory and conversation exports. Joshua explicitly authorized credential transfer; implementation uses the authenticated encrypted package described in `setup/github-sync/README.md`; plaintext secrets and its unlock key stay outside GitHub and Drive. Cloud authentication and native browser sessions still require separate verification.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

## developer

This guide is the shared machine reference for Codex, Claude Code, and Antigravity. The tools are installed once for the Windows user and are not duplicated inside each AI runtime.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Installed tools and locations\\r\\n\\r\\nWinGet package directories are versioned/managed by WinGet. Prefer calling the command by name through PATH. These paths confirm where the current packages live; a later package update may change a folder name.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Command | Current version | Current executable |\\r\\n|---|---|---|\\r\\n| `uv` | 0.12.23 | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\Microsoft\\\\WinGet\\\\Packages\\\\astral-sh.uv_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\\\uv.exe` |\\r\\n| `yt-dlp` | 2026.08.19 | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\Microsoft\\\\WinGet\\\\Packages\\\\yt-dlp.yt-dlp_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\\\yt-dlp.exe` |\\r\\n| `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay` | N-126374-g089a48eb36-20260831 | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\Microsoft\\\\WinGet\\\\Packages\\\\yt-dlp.FFmpeg_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\\\ffmpeg-N-126374-g089a48eb36-win64-gpl\\\\bin\\\\` |\\r\\n| `deno` | 2.9.7 | `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\Microsoft\\\\WinGet\\\\Packages\\\\DenoLand.Deno_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\\\\deno.exe` |\\r\\n\\r\\nThe saved Windows user PATH already contains these package directories. All four commands were verified by name after reloading PATH. The original failure was a stale PATH inherited by already-running Codex, not a missing install. Restarting an AI application gives its new terminals the refreshed environment.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Check access in any runtime\\r\\n\\r\\nRun in that runtime\'s PowerShell terminal:\\r\\n\\r\\n```powershell\\r\\nGet-Command uv, yt-dlp, ffmpeg, ffprobe, ffplay, deno -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |\\r\\n  Select-Object Name, Source\\r\\nuv --version\\r\\nyt-dlp --version\\r\\nffmpeg -version | Select-Object -First 1\\r\\nffprobe -version | Select-Object -First 1\\r\\ndeno --version | Select-Object -First 1\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the commands are installed but are not found, first refresh only the current PowerShell process from the saved Windows PATH:\\r\\n\\r\\n```powershell\\r\\n$machinePath = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable(\'Path\', \'Machine\')\\r\\n$userPath = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable(\'Path\', \'User\')\\r\\n$env:Path = \\"$userPath;$machinePath\\"\\r\\nGet-Command uv, yt-dlp, ffmpeg, ffprobe, ffplay, deno\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nIf that works, restart the affected AI application so its future terminals inherit the environment. Do not reinstall the tools or change persistent PATH just because one already-open shell cannot see them. If the refreshed shell still cannot find a command, inspect `winget list --id <package-id> --exact` and the executable at the table\'s current package path before making changes.\\r\\n\\r\\n## What each tool is for\\r\\n\\r\\n- `uv` creates and manages isolated Python project environments and dependencies. Use `uv add package-name` from the intended code project when that project should depend on a library; do not install every library globally. Use `uv run ...` to run a command in that project environment.\\r\\n- `yt-dlp` retrieves available video metadata and captions, and can retrieve media when specifically needed. For a first caption check, use `yt-dlp --list-subs <URL>`; use `--skip-download` when retrieving subtitles only. Do not assume a website exposes captions or that a successful install grants account access.\\r\\n- `ffmpeg` converts, trims, extracts and processes audio/video. `ffprobe` inspects media metadata. `ffplay` plays media for inspection.\\r\\n- `deno` is a JavaScript/TypeScript runtime. yt-dlp detects it as its JavaScript challenge runtime for supported YouTube extraction; ordinary tasks do not need to invoke it directly.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Python library scope\\r\\n\\r\\nPython packages installed in one interpreter are not automatically shared with the others. Codex\'s managed Python bundle includes `pypdf`, `pdfplumber`, `Pillow`, and `lxml`; the regular user Python 3.12 includes `requests`. Claude Code and Antigravity projects should check their own interpreter and project lockfile. Add only required missing packages to that project\'s isolated environment with uv. Do not modify Codex\'s managed runtime or put virtual environments in the Obsidian vault.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Instructions for the three AI tools\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Codex:** repository rules are in `AGENTS.md`. Use the shared CLI paths and PATH recovery above; restart Codex when its inherited PATH is stale.\\r\\n- **Claude Code:** repository rules are in `CLAUDE.md`. Use the same user-installed executables; project Python dependencies belong in that project\'s environment.\\r\\n- **Antigravity:** workspace rules are in `GEMINI.md` and `AGENTS.md`. Use the same user-installed executables; if it cannot see them, refresh/restart its terminal/application before considering an install.\\r\\n\\r\\nThese tools are shared at the Windows user level. Do not create copies in `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.gemini/`, or inside the vault. Per-project Python packages remain isolated in each project\'s environment.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"7333e7","wall_time_seconds":0.275266,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":455,"output":"# Jay 2: Global Router and Machine Instructions (Antigravity & Codex)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe canonical workspace and command center on this machine is:\\r\\n`C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\dev\\\\ai-os`\\r\\n\\r\\nWhenever an agent starts or operates without an explicit project folder selected, treat `C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\dev\\\\ai-os` as the authoritative workspace root and command center.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Identity and Mission\\r\\nYou are Jay 2, Joshua\'s executive assistant and command center operator. text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/pyproject.toml\n+[project]\n+name = \"youtube-transcripts-local\"\n+version = \"0.1.0\"\n+description = \"Save complete available YouTube captions as timestamped Markdown\"\n+requires-python = \">=3.11\"\n+dependencies = [\"youtube-transcript-api>=1.2.4,<2\"]\n+\n+[project.scripts]\n+youtube-transcript = \"transcript:main\"\n+\n+[build-system]\n+requires = [\"setuptools>=68\"]\n+build-backend = \"setuptools.build_meta\"\n+\n+[tool.setuptools]\n+py-modules = [\"transcript\"]\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/transcript.py\n+\"\"\"Caption-only collection. No media downloads, credentials, paid APIs or summarization.\"\"\"\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import argparse\n+import hashlib\n+import html\n+import json\n+import os\n+from pathlib import Path\n+import re\n+import shutil\n+import subprocess\n+import sys\n+import tempfile\n+from datetime import datetime, timezone\n+from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse\n+\n+\n+def video_id(value: str) -> str:\n+    if re.fullmatch(r\"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}\", value):\n+        return value\n+    parsed = urlparse(value)\n+    host = (parsed.hostname or \"\").lower()\n+    if host in {\"youtu.be\", \"www.youtu.be\"}:\n+        candidate = parsed.path.strip(\"/\").split(\"/\")[0]\n+    elif host in {\"youtube.com\", \"www.youtube.com\", \"m.youtube.com\", \"music.youtube.com\"}:\n+        candidate = parse_qs(parsed.query).get(\"v\", [\"\"])[0]\n+        if not candidate and parsed.path.startswith((\"/shorts/\", \"/embed/\", \"/live/\")):\n+            candidate = parsed.path.split(\"/\")[2]\n+    else:\n+        raise ValueError(\"Supply a YouTube video URL or its 11-character video ID\")\n+    if not re.fullmatch(r\"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}\", candidate):\n+        raise ValueError(\"Invalid or missing YouTube video ID\")\n+    return candidate\n+\n+\n+def executable(name: str) -> str | None:\n+    found = shutil.which(name)\n+    if found:\n+        return found\n+    if os.name != \"nt\":\n+        return None\n+    import winreg\n+    for root, key in [(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, \"Environment\"),\n+                      (winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r\"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Session Manager\\Environment\")]:\n+        try:\n+            with winreg.OpenKey(root, key) as handle:\n+                saved = winreg.QueryValueEx(handle, \"Path\")[0]\n+            found = shutil.which(name, path=os.path.expandvars(saved))\n+            if found:\n+                return found\n+        except OSError:\n+            pass\n+    packages = Path(os.environ.get(\"LOCALAPPDATA\", \"\")) / \"Microsoft/WinGet/Packages\"\n+    package = {\"yt-dlp\": \"yt-dlp.yt-dlp_*\", \"deno\": \"DenoLand.Deno_*\"}.get(name)\n+    if package:\n+        for directory in packages.glob(package):\n+            for candidate in directory.rglob(name + \".exe\"):\n+                return str(candidate)\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def seconds(value: str) -> float:\n+    h, m, s = value.replace(\",\", \".\").split(\":\")\n+    return int(h) * 3600 + int(m) * 60 + float(s)\n+\n+\n+def parse_subtitles(content: str) -> list[dict]:\n+    \"\"\"Read SRT/VTT, preserving multiline cues and stripping only markup.\"\"\"\n+    cues = []\n+    pattern = re.compile(r\"(\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2}[,.]\\d{3}|\\d{2}:\\d{2}[.]\\d{3})\\s*-->\\s*(\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2}[,.]\\d{3}|\\d{2}:\\d{2}[.]\\d{3})[^\\n]*\\n([^\\n]*(?:\\n(?!\\s*\\n)[^\\n]*)*)\")\n+    for match in pattern.finditer(content.replace(\"\\r\\n\", \"\\n\")):\n+        start, end = match.group(1), match.group(2)\n+        start = \"00:\" + start if start.count(\":\") == 1 else start\n+        end = \"00:\" + end if end.count(\":\") == 1 else end\n+        text = html.unescape(re.sub(r\"<[^>]+>\", \"\", match.group(3))).strip()\n+        if text:\n+            cues.append({\"start\": seconds(start), \"duration\": max(0, seconds(end)-seconds(start)), \"text\": text})\n+    return cues\n+\n+\n+def coverage(cues: list[dict], duration: float | None, threshold: float) -> dict:\n+    intervals = sorted((float(c[\"start\"]), float(c[\"start\"])+float(c[\"duration\"])) for c in cues)\n+    gaps, end = [], 0.0\n+    for start, stop in intervals:\n+        if start - end > threshold:\n+            gaps.append({\"start\": end, \"end\": start})\n+        end = max(end, stop)\n+    if duration is not None and duration-end > threshold:\n+        gaps.append({\"start\": end, \"end\": duration})\n+    return {\"cue_count\": len(cues), \"last_caption_end\": end, \"video_duration\": duration,\n+            \"gap_threshold_seconds\": threshold, \"caption_gaps\": gaps,\n+            \"speech_completeness_verified\": False}\n+\n+\n+def stamp(value: float) -> str:\n+    value = max(0, int(value))\n+    return f\"{value//3600:02}:{value//60%60:02}:{value%60:02}\"\n+\n+\n+def markdown_text(cues: list[dict]) -> list[str]:\n+    # VTT rolling captions repeat words across overlapping cues. Remove only\n+    # an exact suffix/prefix token match when cues overlap; retain raw cues.\n+    lines, previous, previous_end = [], [], -1.0\n+    for cue in cues:\n+        words = cue[\"text\"].split()\n+        trim = 0\n+        if cue[\"start\"] < previous_end:\n+            for length in range(min(len(previous), len(words)), 0, -1):\n+                if previous[-length:] == words[:length]:\n+                    trim = length\n+                    break\n+        if words[trim:]:\n+            lines.append(f\"[{stamp(cue['start'])}] {' '.join(words[trim:])}\")\n+        previous, previous_end = words, cue[\"start\"] + cue[\"duration\"]\n+    return lines\n+\n+\n+def fetch_api(ident: str, languages: list[str]) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:\n+    from youtube_transcript_api import YouTubeTranscriptApi\n+    transcript = YouTubeTranscriptApi().list(ident).find_transcript(languages)\n+    result = transcript.fetch()\n+    return result.to_raw_data(), {\"provider\": \"youtube-transcript-api\", \"language\": transcript.language_code,\n+                                 \"automatic_captions\": transcript.is_generated}\n+\n+\n+def fetch_ytdlp(ident: str, languages: list[str]) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:\n+    tool = executable(\"yt-dlp\")\n+    if not tool:\n+        raise RuntimeError(\"yt-dlp is not available on current PATH, saved PATH or WinGet package folders\")\n+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix=\"youtube-captions-\") as temporary:\n+        command = [tool, \"--ignore-config\", \"--skip-download\", \"--write-subs\", \"--write-auto-subs\",\n+                   \"--sub-langs\", \",\".join(languages), \"--sub-format\", \"json3/vtt/srt\", \"--write-info-json\",\n+                   \"--no-playlist\", \"--retries\", \"0\", \"--extractor-retries\", \"0\", \"--socket-timeout\", \"20\",\n+                   \"-o\", str(Path(temporary)/\"captions.%(ext)s\"), f\"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={ident}\"]\n+        deno = executable(\"deno\")\n+        if deno:\n+            command[1:1] = [\"--js-runtimes\", \"deno:\" + deno]\n+        result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120, encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n+        root = Path(temporary)\n+        files = [p for p in root.glob(\"*\") if p.suffix in {\".json3\", \".vtt\", \".srt\"}]\n+        if not files:\n+            raise RuntimeError(f\"yt-dlp returned {result.returncode}; no captions saved. {result.stderr[-1800:]}\")\n+        source = sorted(files)[0]\n+        content = source.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8-sig\")\n+        if source.suffix == \".json3\":\n+            cues = [{\"start\": event[\"tStartMs\"]/1000, \"duration\": event.get(\"dDurationMs\", 0)/1000,\n+                     \"text\": \"\".join(seg.get(\"utf8\", \"\") for seg in event.get(\"segs\", [])).strip()}\n+                    for event in json.loads(content).get(\"events\", []) if event.get(\"segs\")]\n+            cues = [c for c in cues if c[\"text\"]]\n+        else:\n+            cues = parse_subtitles(content)\n+        info_files = list(root.glob(\"*.info.json\"))\n+        info = json.loads(info_files[0].read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) if info_files else {}\n+        return cues, {\"provider\": \"yt-dlp\", \"caption_filename\": source.name,\n+                      \"original_caption_text\": content, \"title\": info.get(\"title\"), \"duration\": info.get(\"duration\"),\n+                      \"automatic_captions\": \"unknown\", \"warning\": result.stderr[-1800:] if result.returncode else None}\n+\n+\n+def main() -> int:\n+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n+    parser.add_argument(\"url\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--out\", type=Path, required=True)\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--lang\", default=\"en\", help=\"Comma-separated preferred caption languages\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--title\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--duration\", type=float, help=\"Known video length in seconds; avoids a metadata request\")\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--gap-seconds\", type=float, default=15)\n+    parser.add_argument(\"--import-srt\", type=Path, help=\"Import already obtained local SRT/VTT without network access\")\n+    args = parser.parse_args()\n+    try:\n+        ident = video_id(args.url)\n+        languages = [v.strip() for v in args.lang.split(\",\") if v.strip()]\n+        if not languages or args.gap_seconds < 0 or (args.duration is not None and args.duration <= 0):\n+            raise ValueError(\"Supply a language, a nonnegative gap threshold and positive duration\")\n+        args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+        base = args.out / f\"{ident}.{languages[0]}\"\n+        raw_path, md_path = Path(str(base)+\".raw.json\"), Path(str(base)+\".md\")\n+        errors = []\n+        if raw_path.exists():\n+            raw = json.loads(raw_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n+            if raw.get(\"video_id\") != ident or raw.get(\"requested_languages\") != languages:\n+                raise ValueError(\"Existing cache provenance differs; choose another output directory\")\n+            cues, provenance = raw[\"cues\"], raw[\"provenance\"]\n+            if args.import_srt:\n+                imported_hash = hashlib.sha256(args.import_srt.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n+                if provenance.get(\"import_sha256\") != imported_hash:\n+                    raise ValueError(\"Existing cache differs from imported source; choose another output directory\")\n+        else:\n+            if args.import_srt:\n+                content = args.import_srt.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8-sig\")\n+                cues = parse_subtitles(content)\n+                provenance = {\"provider\": \"local-subtitle-import\", \"source_file\": str(args.import_srt.resolve()),\n+                              \"import_sha256\": hashlib.sha256(args.import_srt.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),\n+                              \"original_caption_text\": content, \"automatic_captions\": \"unknown\"}\n+            else:\n+                try:\n+                    cues, provenance = fetch_api(ident, languages)\n+                except Exception as exc:\n+                    errors.append(f\"youtube-transcript-api: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n+                    cues, provenance = fetch_ytdlp(ident, languages)\n+            if not cues:\n+                raise ValueError(\"No caption cues obtained\")\n+            raw = {\"video_id\": ident, \"requested_languages\": languages,\n+                   \"retrieved_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), \"provenance\": provenance,\n+                   \"failed_attempts\": errors, \"cues\": cues}\n+            raw_path.write_text(json.dumps(raw, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+        report = coverage(cues, args.duration or provenance.get(\"duration\"), args.gap_seconds)\n+        report_path = Path(str(base)+\".coverage.json\")\n+        report_path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+        heading = args.title or provenance.get(\"title\") or f\"YouTube transcript {ident}\"\n+        notes = [f\"# {heading}\", \"\", f\"Source: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={ident}\",\n+                 f\"Caption source: {provenance['provider']}\", f\"Retrieved: {raw['retrieved_at']}\",\n+                 f\"Raw SHA-256: {hashlib.sha256(raw_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}\", \"\",\n+                 \"## Coverage\", \"\", f\"Saved all {len(cues)} available caption cues without summarization.\",\n+                 \"Captions may contain recognition errors. Speech completeness is not independently verified.\",\n+                 \"Temporal gaps indicate absent caption cues and may be silence, not necessarily missing speech.\"]\n+        if report[\"video_duration\"] is None:\n+            notes.append(\"Video duration unknown: coverage at the end cannot be assessed. Supply --duration to assess it.\")\n+        if report[\"caption_gaps\"]:\n+            notes += [f\"- Caption gap: {stamp(g['start'])} to {stamp(g['end'])}\" for g in report[\"caption_gaps\"]]\n+        else:\n+            notes.append(f\"No caption gaps longer than {args.gap_seconds:g} seconds detected in the assessed range.\")\n+        notes += [\"\", \"## Transcript\", \"\"] + markdown_text(cues)\n+        rendered = \"\\n\".join(notes)+\"\\n\"\n+        if md_path.exists() and md_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") != rendered:\n+            raise ValueError(\"Markdown already exists with different content; choose another output directory\")\n+        md_path.write_text(rendered, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+        Path(str(base)+\".SHA256SUMS\").write_text(\"\".join(f\"{hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}  {p.name}\\n\" for p in [raw_path, md_path, report_path]), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+        print(f\"Saved {len(cues)} cues: {md_path.resolve()}\")\n+        print(f\"Caption gaps: {len(report['caption_gaps'])}; speech completeness not independently verified\")\n+        return 0\n+    except Exception as exc:\n+        print(f\"Transcript not completed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        return 1\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/test_transcript.py\n+import unittest\n+from transcript import coverage, markdown_text, parse_subtitles, video_id\n+\n+\n+class TranscriptTests(unittest.TestCase):\n+    def test_urls_and_reject_other_domains(self):\n+        for url in [\"7xTGNNLPyMI\", \"https://youtu.be/7xTGNNLPyMI?t=1\", \"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7xTGNNLPyMI\"]:\n+            self.assertEqual(video_id(url), \"7xTGNNLPyMI\")\n+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\n+            video_id(\"https://example.com/watch?v=7xTGNNLPyMI\")\n+\n+    def test_multiline_rolling_caption_and_coverage(self):\n+        source = \"1\\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:03,000\\nhello\\nworld\\n\\n2\\n00:00:02,000 --> 00:00:05,000\\nworld again\\n\\n3\\n00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,000\\nworld again\\n\"\n+        cues = parse_subtitles(source)\n+        self.assertEqual(len(cues), 3)\n+        self.assertEqual(markdown_text(cues), [\"[00:00:00] hello world\", \"[00:00:02] again\", \"[00:00:30] world again\"])\n+        self.assertEqual(coverage(cues, 60, 15)[\"caption_gaps\"], [{\"start\": 5.0, \"end\": 30.0}, {\"start\": 31.0, \"end\": 60}])\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    unittest.main()\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/README.md\n+# Save available YouTube captions in one command\n+\n+This free Python CLI saves timestamped Markdown, original caption cues in JSON, provenance, SHA-256 hashes and a coverage report. It does not summarize. It tries youtube-transcript-api once, then your shared yt-dlp install once. It never downloads video/audio or exports browser credentials.\n+\n+From this project directory:\n+\n+```powershell\n+uv run youtube-transcript \"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7xTGNNLPyMI\" --out \"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\raw\\inbox\\youtube\\7xTGNNLPyMI\"\n+```\n+\n+uv creates the isolated project environment and installs only the declared free dependency. Dependencies stay outside the Obsidian vault. The first run needs network access to obtain that dependency; later runs reuse it.\n+\n+Options: `--lang en` (or comma-separated preferences), `--title \"Known title\"`, `--duration 12345` (seconds), `--gap-seconds 15`. Supplying known duration assesses the tail without requesting metadata separately. Without it, duration is available only if yt-dlp supplied metadata.\n+\n+If YouTube blocks caption access, the command fails clearly rather than inventing a transcript. Import a local subtitle file obtained legitimately instead:\n+\n+```powershell\n+uv run youtube-transcript \"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7xTGNNLPyMI\" --out \"C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\raw\\inbox\\youtube\\7xTGNNLPyMI\" --import-srt \"C:\\path\\captions.srt\" --duration 12345\n+```\n+\n+SRT and VTT are supported. Local imports use no network. An existing raw JSON cache is reused; edited transcripts are not overwritten. Use a new output directory to fetch a fresh source generation or change metadata. Original cues remain intact in JSON. Markdown removes exact repeated words only in overlapping rolling captions.\n+\n+Coverage reports identify timestamp intervals without captions. Such intervals can be silence. Successful extraction means all cues returned by the caption source were saved; it does not independently prove every spoken word was captioned. Automatic captions may contain mistakes. No paid APIs, proxies, subscriptions or automatic audio transcription are used.\n+\n+Run focused tests: `uv run python -m unittest -v`.\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/chrome-extension/manifest.json\n+{\n+  \"manifest_version\": 3,\n+  \"name\": \"YouTube captions to Markdown\",\n+  \"version\": \"1.0.0\",\n+  \"description\": \"Save available captions from the current YouTube video as timestamped Markdown.\",\n+  \"permissions\": [\"activeTab\", \"scripting\", \"downloads\"],\n+  \"action\": {\"default_popup\": \"popup.html\"}\n+}\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/chrome-extension/popup.html\n+<!doctype html>\n+<html><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><title>Save YouTube transcript</title></head>\n+<body style=\"width:320px;font:14px system-ui;padding:12px\">\n+<button id=\"save\">Save full available captions as Markdown</button>\n+<p id=\"status\" role=\"status\">Uses the current YouTube video. No paid service.</p>\n+<script src=\"popup.js\"></script>\n+</body></html>\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/chrome-extension/popup.js\n+document.querySelector('#save').addEventListener('click', async () => {\n+  const status = document.querySelector('#status');\n+  const button = document.querySelector('#save');\n+  button.disabled = true;\n+  status.textContent = 'Reading available caption track…';\n+  try {\n+    const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({active: true, currentWindow: true});\n+    const url = new URL(tab.url);\n+    if (!['www.youtube.com', 'youtube.com', 'm.youtube.com'].includes(url.hostname) || !url.searchParams.get('v')) {\n+      throw new Error('Open a YouTube watch page first.');\n+    }\n+    const id = url.searchParams.get('v');\n+    const [{result}] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({target: {tabId: tab.id}, world: 'MAIN', func: collectCaptions, args: [id]});\n+    if (!result || result.error) throw new Error(result?.error || 'No transcript returned.');\n+    const blob = new Blob([result.markdown], {type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8'});\n+    const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n+    await chrome.downloads.download({url: objectUrl, filename: `youtube-transcripts/${id}.md`, saveAs: false, conflictAction: 'uniquify'});\n+    // Keep the object URL alive until the popup closes so Chrome can read it.\n+    status.textContent = `Saved ${result.count} caption segments to Downloads/youtube-transcripts. ${result.method}`;\n+  } catch (error) {\n+    status.textContent = `Could not save: ${error.message}`;\n+  } finally {\n+    button.disabled = false;\n+  }\n+});\n+\n+async function collectCaptions(expectedId) {\n+  const timestamp = value => {\n+    value = Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));\n+    return [Math.floor(value / 3600), Math.floor(value / 60) % 60, value % 60].map(v => String(v).padStart(2, '0')).join(':');\n+  };\n+  const currentId = () => new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('v');\n+  const assertCurrent = () => {if (currentId() !== expectedId) throw new Error('Video changed during extraction. Try again.');};\n+  try {\n+    assertCurrent();\n+    let cues = [], method = '', language = 'unknown', duration = null, failures = [];\n+    const player = document.getElementById('movie_player');\n+    const response = typeof player?.getPlayerResponse === 'function' ? player.getPlayerResponse() : null;\n+    const title = response?.videoDetails?.title || document.querySelector('h1.ytd-watch-metadata')?.textContent?.trim() || document.title;\n+    if (response?.videoDetails?.videoId && response.videoDetails.videoId !== expectedId) throw new Error('Player data belongs to another video. Reload and try again.');\n+    duration = Number(response?.videoDetails?.lengthSeconds) || null;\n+    const tracks = response?.captions?.playerCaptionsTracklistRenderer?.captionTracks || [];\n+    const track = tracks.find(t => t.languageCode === 'en' && t.kind !== 'asr') || tracks.find(t => t.languageCode === 'en') || tracks[0];\n+    if (track?.baseUrl) {\n+      try {\n+        const captionUrl = new URL(track.baseUrl);\n+        if (captionUrl.hostname !== 'www.youtube.com' && !captionUrl.hostname.endsWith('.youtube.com')) throw new Error('Unexpected caption host.');\n+        captionUrl.searchParams.set('fmt', 'json3');\n+        const fetched = await fetch(captionUrl, {credentials: 'same-origin', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});\n+        if (!fetched.ok) throw new Error(`Caption request returned HTTP ${fetched.status}.`);\n+        const data = await fetched.json();\n+        cues = (data.events || []).filter(e => Array.isArray(e.segs)).map(e => ({start: e.tStartMs / 1000, duration: (e.dDurationMs || 0) / 1000, text: e.segs.map(s => s.utf8 || '').join('').trim()})).filter(c => c.text && Number.isFinite(c.start));\n+        if (!cues.length) throw new Error('Caption endpoint returned no text.');\n+        method = 'Complete returned caption track';\n+        language = `${track.languageCode || 'unknown'} (${track.kind === 'asr' ? 'automatic' : 'provided'})`;\n+      } catch (error) {failures.push(error.message);}\n+    } else failures.push('Player did not expose a caption track.');\n+    if (!cues.length) {\n+      let rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('ytd-transcript-segment-renderer')];\n+      if (!rows.length) {\n+        const buttons = [...document.querySelectorAll('button, [role=\"button\"]')];\n+        const show = buttons.find(b => /show transcript/i.test(b.textContent || b.getAttribute('aria-label') || ''));\n+        if (show) show.click();\n+        else {\n+          const more = document.querySelector('ytd-text-inline-expander #expand');\n+          if (more) more.click();\n+          const expanded = [...document.querySelectorAll('button, [role=\"button\"]')].find(b => /show transcript/i.test(b.textContent || b.getAttribute('aria-label') || ''));\n+          if (expanded) expanded.click();\n+        }\n+        await new Promise(resolve => {\n+          const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {if (document.querySelector('ytd-transcript-segment-renderer')) {observer.disconnect(); clearTimeout(timeout); resolve();}});\n+          const timeout = setTimeout(() => {observer.disconnect(); resolve();}, 6000);\n+          observer.observe(document.body, {childList: true, subtree: true});\n+          if (document.querySelector('ytd-transcript-segment-renderer')) {observer.disconnect(); clearTimeout(timeout); resolve();}\n+        });\n+        rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('ytd-transcript-segment-renderer')];\n+      }\n+      cues = rows.map(row => {\n+        const text = row.querySelector('.segment-text')?.textContent?.trim();\n+        const raw = row.querySelector('.segment-timestamp')?.textContent?.trim();\n+        if (!text || !/^\\d{1,3}:\\d{2}(?::\\d{2})?$/.test(raw || '')) return null;\n+        const parts = raw.split(':').map(Number);\n+        return {start: parts.reduce((total, part) => total * 60 + part, 0), duration: 0, text};\n+      }).filter(Boolean);\n+      method = 'Transcript panel DOM fallback; full coverage unverified (panel may be virtualized)';\n+    }\n+    assertCurrent();\n+    if (!cues.length) throw new Error(`No captions obtained. ${failures.join(' ')} No empty transcript saved.`);\n+    if (cues.some((c, i) => i && c.start < cues[i-1].start)) throw new Error('Caption timestamps are out of order; no file saved.');\n+    const gapNotes = [];\n+    let end = 0;\n+    for (const cue of cues) {if (cue.start-end > 15) gapNotes.push(`- Caption interval without cues: ${timestamp(end)} to ${timestamp(cue.start)}`); end = Math.max(end, cue.start+cue.duration);}\n+    if (duration && duration-end > 15) gapNotes.push(`- Tail without cues: ${timestamp(end)} to ${timestamp(duration)}`);\n+    const lines = [`# ${title}`, '', `Source: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${expectedId}`, `Extracted: ${new Date().toISOString()}`, `Method: ${method}`, `Language: ${language}`, `Caption segments: ${cues.length}`, '', '## Coverage', '', 'All returned cues are preserved below without summarization. Automatic captions can contain errors. Missing spoken content is not independently verified. Timestamp gaps may be silence.', ...(gapNotes.length ? gapNotes : ['No gaps longer than 15 seconds detected within the assessed caption range.']), ...(duration ? [] : ['Video duration unavailable; tail coverage cannot be assessed.']), ...(failures.length ? ['', 'Track lookup notes:', ...failures.map(f => `- ${f}`)] : []), '', '## Transcript', '', ...cues.map(c => `[${timestamp(c.start)}] ${c.text}`), ''];\n+    return {markdown: lines.join('\\n'), count: cues.length, method};\n+  } catch (error) {return {error: error.message};}\n+}\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/youtube-transcripts/chrome-extension/README.md\n+# Save captions from the current Chrome video\n+\n+One-time setup: open `chrome://extensions`, enable Developer mode, choose Load unpacked, select this `chrome-extension` folder, then pin the extension.\n+\n+Open a YouTube watch page and click the extension, then its Save button. The timestamped Markdown downloads to `Downloads/youtube-transcripts/`. No Python, terminal, paid API, broad browsing permissions or external service is needed. Chrome controls installation; this project does not install the extension automatically.\n+\n+The normal path reads the current YouTube player's caption track and retrieves its JSON caption data quietly without opening the transcript panel. If YouTube exposes no usable track, it tries the page's Show transcript button and waits at most six seconds for rendered caption rows. That fallback may visibly expand the description or transcript panel. It does not defeat CAPTCHA, access restrictions or authentication failures.\n+\n+The file explicitly distinguishes a full returned caption track from a DOM fallback whose completeness is unknown. Transcript panels may be virtualized, so a DOM result is not certified as the whole video. The extension rejects empty results and wrong-video player data. All returned cues are preserved, including any repeated rolling caption text. Gaps above 15 seconds are listed and may reflect silence rather than missing speech.\n+\n+YouTube can change its player and transcript DOM. Player-caption access and selector compatibility must be verified in your Chrome after installation; static syntax checks alone do not prove the current site works. The popup must remain open while it reads captions. Existing downloads are kept with unique names.\n*** End Patch"));
